# BAYONETTA
# Chart baselines: normal F34; jump F14 (F13-15); ledge roll F31.
# Assumes recognition at option F1, grounded/ready, correct facing and spacing.
# Ground attacks assume shield is already released; OOS uses the block helper.
# Charged smash release and hitbox contact require in-game calibration.
# Down-smash branches choose front/rear at entry; position for the roll endpoint.
# Auto-react is immediate; ordinary dodges do not use the ledge timings.
# Automatic admission is grounded; aerial tapes may finish after takeoff.

# DUCK HUNT

# Ground attacks assume shield is already released; OOS uses the block helper.
# Charged smash release and hitbox contact require in-game calibration.
# Down-smash branches choose front/rear at entry; position for the roll endpoint.
# Auto-react is immediate; ordinary dodges do not use the ledge timings.
# Automatic admission is grounded; aerial tapes may finish after takeoff.

# IKE
# Chart baselines: normal F34; jump F14 (F13-15); ledge roll F31.
# Assumes recognition at option F1, grounded/ready, correct facing and spacing.
# Ground attacks assume shield is already released; OOS uses the block helper.
# Charged smash release and hitbox contact require in-game calibration.
# Down-smash branches choose front/rear at entry; position for the roll endpoint.
# Auto-react is immediate; ordinary dodges do not use the ledge timings.
# Automatic admission is grounded; aerial tapes may finish after takeoff.

# IVYSAUR
# Chart baselines: normal F34; jump F14 (F13-15); ledge roll F31.
# Assumes recognition at option F1, grounded/ready, correct facing and spacing.
# Ground attacks assume shield is already released; OOS uses the block helper.
# Charged smash release and hitbox contact require in-game calibration.
# Down-smash branches choose front/rear at entry; position for the roll endpoint.
# Auto-react is immediate; ordinary dodges do not use the ledge timings.
# Automatic admission is grounded; aerial tapes may finish after takeoff.

# KING DEDEDE

# Ground attacks assume shield is already released; OOS uses the block helper.
# Charged smash release and hitbox contact require in-game calibration.
# Down-smash branches choose front/rear at entry; position for the roll endpoint.
# Auto-react is immediate; ordinary dodges do not use the ledge timings.
# Automatic admission is grounded; aerial tapes may finish after takeoff.
